# Elections on IRS Forms

Catalog of taxpayer elections made on, with, or by filing the forms in the "IRS Forms" folder (1,017 PDFs), cross-checked against the instructions and publications in "IRS English Forms Instructions Publications (2026-09-30)". Code citations were checked against `usc26.xml` and regulation citations against `CFR-title-26`.

| Bin | Elections |
|---|---|
| Bin 1 — Must choose among boxes | 101 |
| Bin 2 — Implicit elections | 201 |
| Bin 3 — Single checkbox to elect in or out | 197 |
| Bin 4 — Elections made by attaching a statement | 135 |
| Bin 5 — Everything else | 242 |
| **Total** | **876** |
| Appendix — reported on a form but made outside the cataloged forms | 90 |

**Conventions.** Each row is the place where the election is actually made. "Also at" lists (a) the same election on variant or related forms (e.g., 1040-SR, 1040-NR), and (b) boxes or lines elsewhere that only report the election, marked "(reports the election)." Where the same legal election is made by different mechanisms on different forms, each mechanism has its own row. Revocations are binned by mechanism, like any other election. "Source" shows whether the election appears on the face of the form, only in the instructions, or only in a publication. "none located" means no supporting section was found in the Code or CFR files.

**Not covered:** 97 forms in the folder are dynamic XFA PDFs (e.g., Forms 8980–8989, 8955-SSA, 8947, 8963) with no extractable text on their face. They are reflected here only to the extent their instructions or publications describe an election.

## Bin 1 — Must choose among boxes

The taxpayer must pick one of several mutually exclusive options by checking boxes (e.g., Form 1040 Filing Status; election vs. revocation on Form 5768).

## Bin 2 — Implicit elections

Made by what the taxpayer does elsewhere on the return rather than through a dedicated election box (e.g., standard vs. itemized deduction; claiming a credit by filing the credit form; deemed elections).

## Bin 4 — Elections made by attaching a statement

No box on the form; the election is made by attaching a statement to the return (or an amended return), sometimes alongside a line entry. Elections made by checking a box that also requires a supporting statement stay in Bin 3.

## Bin 5 — Everything else

The form itself is the election (e.g., 2553, 8832, 8716); dollar-amount or code entries; consents, agreements and signatures; revocations made by filing a new or marked form; Form 3115 DCN-based elections; and non-tax/procedural choices.

## Appendix — Elections reported on a form but made outside the cataloged forms

These boxes or lines report an election that is not itself made on any form in the catalog (e.g., elections made in bond documents, plan documents, separate statements the instructions do not tie to a cataloged return, or forms outside the set). They have no row above to attach to, so the reporting location is shown under "Reported at."

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Cafeteria plan salary reduction election (HSA contributions; dependent care) - reported | Form W-2, box 12, code W; box 10; Form W-2AS, box 12, code W; Form W-2GU, box 12, code W; Form W-2VI, box 12, code W | Code W includes amounts the employee elected to contribute to an HSA through a section 125 plan; box 10 includes dependent care amounts elected under a cafeteria plan. | IRC §§ 125(a), (d)(1)(B), 129, 223 | Treas. Reg. § 1.125-4 |
| 2 | Deferral election under a section 409A nonqualified deferred compensation plan - reported | Form W-2, box 12, code Y; Form W-2AS, box 12, code Y; Form W-2GU, box 12, code Y; Form W-2VI, box 12, code Y | Code Y reports current-year deferrals the employee elected under a 409A plan. | IRC § 409A(a)(4) | Treas. Reg. § 1.409A-2(a) |
